README.md:39: trailing whitespace.

README.md:40: trailing whitespace.
+RIPOSTE lets you protect selected images, sort a folder, or explicitly do both. Sorting separates images and videos, rebuilds images with date-only metadata, and retains original files privately for undo. Protection uses selected local models; exact reviewed outputs can be packaged for sharing. Direct commands and the interactive terminal share the same operations. The browser remains available; the workshop remains optional.

+## Terminal revision 0.5
README.md:43: trailing whitespace.
+
README.md:44: trailing whitespace.
+Foreground-only `goks` is now the default. The host owns the background in every scene. Interactive results offer Overview / Details / JSON with `t`. Enter selects images, `i` inspects, `p` configures protection, and F1 explains the current screen. `f` expands advanced protection settings without resetting them.

+## Install, then open the menu
README.md:49: trailing whitespace.
+
README.md:50: trailing whitespace.
+Keep the application separate from image and model folders. Python 3.11+ is required.
README.md:51: trailing whitespace.
+Workspace selection, previews and execution reject the application folder, its
README.md:52: trailing whitespace.
+parents and its subfolders across the CLI, terminal and API. A source checkout
README.md:53: trailing whitespace.
+protects the RIPOSTE project root; a wheel installation protects the loaded
README.md:54: trailing whitespace.
+`riposte` package directory. Independent sibling image/results folders are allowed.
README.md:55: trailing whitespace.
+Linked folders and junctions remain rejected. If a library was previously placed
README.md:56: trailing whitespace.
+inside the application, move the whole library, including its private `.riposte`
README.md:57: trailing whitespace.
+or `.shroud` state and `.riposte-operation.lease`, to a separate folder on the
README.md:58: trailing whitespace.
+same filesystem while no operation is running, then preview it there. See the
README.md:59: trailing whitespace.
+[safeguard evidence and layout boundary](evidence/application-roots/README.md).

+Direct commands and interactive forms call the same operation service. The interface does not simulate an engine, shell out to a pretend CLI, or execute the text displayed in the command lens.

README.md:179: trailing whitespace.
+| Sort + RIPOSTE | **Sort + RIPOSTE** previews sorting and protection, then requires `REPLACE` | `riposte sort-riposte ROOT --all --vae MODEL`; add `--apply --plan-id PLAN_ID` from the combined preview to run both |
README.md:180: trailing whitespace.
+
README.md:181: trailing whitespace.
+Standalone RIPOSTE preserves original files and folders. `ROOT` holds its application state and reviewed exports; inputs may be elsewhere. It creates date-only clean snapshots for resumable protection without reorganizing the input library. Directory inputs are recursive; the terminal browser accepts multiple selected files and folders. For existing catalog images use `--image ID` or `--all` with filters instead of `--input`. Unrelated additions no longer block publishing, reviewing or packaging selected catalog images.
README.md:182: trailing whitespace.
+
README.md:183: trailing whitespace.
+**Every sort apply requires a reviewed preview ID.** Ordinary previews now show exact per-file destinations and bind source identity, content, date/sidecar policy, catalogs and private title records. Reinspect after adding, removing or changing inputs or settings. Combined sorting uses its **top-level** `plan_id`, which also binds the protection recipe, selection, device and model paths; the nested sorting ID cannot authorize both operations. Terminal and browser confirmation carry the displayed ID automatically. Direct callers must pass it explicitly. Pre-existing stale or missing consent is rejected before creating workspace state.
README.md:184: trailing whitespace.
+
README.md:185: trailing whitespace.
+Sort retains the established reconstruction and source-replacement behavior, including explicit sidecar consent. Repeating it processes new additions and leaves verified clean masters alone. The combined workflow sorts `ROOT` and protects matching catalog images, including newly sorted additions. Model preflight happens before replacement; an incomplete sort stops protection. After successful sorting, protection can be retried separately. Existing `organize`, `publish`, `protect`, and expert commands remain available. Candidate creation never grants visual approval or verified protection efficacy.

README.md:194: trailing whitespace.

README.md:235: trailing whitespace.
+
README.md:236: trailing whitespace.
+**Preview is read-only.** It shows destination folders, image/video counts and
README.md:237: trailing whitespace.
+blockers without creating application state. **Undo applies to sorts recorded
README.md:238: trailing whitespace.
+by this version**, including partially completed sorts. Earlier sorts that
README.md:239: trailing whitespace.
+discarded originals have no recovery record. Each new sort retains original
README.md:240: trailing whitespace.
+bytes, names and modified times under `.riposte/sort-history` (or legacy
README.md:241: trailing whitespace.
+`.shroud/sort-history`). This private history includes original metadata and
README.md:242: trailing whitespace.
+uses additional disk space, including full video copies. Keep it with the
README.md:243: trailing whitespace.
+library; deleting it removes the ability to undo. It never enters publishing
README.md:244: trailing whitespace.
+packs. Repeated undo walks backward through recorded sorts; a no-change sort
README.md:245: trailing whitespace.
+does not replace the last recovery record.
README.md:246: trailing whitespace.
+
README.md:247: trailing whitespace.
+Undo checks the complete operation before restoring files. Occupied original
README.md:248: trailing whitespace.
+paths, changed sorted files, damaged backups and changed catalogs block it;
README.md:249: trailing whitespace.
+files added elsewhere remain untouched. Protection outputs and reviews remain
README.md:250: trailing whitespace.
+in place, although a removed catalog image must be sorted again before a batch
README.md:251: trailing whitespace.
+that depends on that image can resume. Already created publishing packs remain
README.md:252: trailing whitespace.
+unchanged. Legacy image paths remain readable; the next explicit sort moves
README.md:253: trailing whitespace.
+them into `img` while preserving image IDs, dates and bytes.
README.md:254: trailing whitespace.
+
README.md:255: trailing whitespace.
+Workspace operations now hold an OS lease on the empty root file
README.md:256: trailing whitespace.
+`.riposte-operation.lease`. Keep that file in place. If a current-version worker
README.md:257: trailing whitespace.
+is killed, the next explicit operation can reclaim its matching abandoned marker
README.md:258: trailing whitespace.
+after acquiring the lease; undo still requires its preview and integrity checks.
README.md:259: trailing whitespace.
+Recovery never guesses from a PID or a lock's age. Unidentified legacy/empty lock
README.md:260: trailing whitespace.
+markers remain blocked, and unsupported locking or atomic publication fails
README.md:261: trailing whitespace.
+closed. Older versions may report the new root file as an unsupported input.

+## The ASCII experience
README.md:265: trailing whitespace.
+
README.md:266: trailing whitespace.
+COUNTERSTROKE is an authored depth-resolved lamellar image-plane material with moving hinges, travelling light and a reflected stroke. It is drawn from ordinary ASCII cells by Rich, not from terminal bitmaps, web views, a font pack or a pre-rendered movie. The entrance is immediately interactive: no artificial loading countdown or minimum animation duration.
README.md:267: trailing whitespace.
+
README.md:268: trailing whitespace.
+Working screens have different compositions: selected images, editable protection fields, real batch outputs, explicit source-replacement confirmation, operation activity and full result inspection. Colour ASCII previews help identify an image; **they are not substitutes for full-resolution visual review**. Press `v` in an image inspector to hand audited PNG bytes to a local image viewer. The handoff does not approve the image.
README.md:269: trailing whitespace.
+
README.md:270: trailing whitespace.
+| Key | Behaviour |

+| --- | --- |
README.md:272: trailing whitespace.
+| Arrows or `j` / `k` | Move focus |
README.md:273: trailing whitespace.
+| Enter | Activate the highlighted action; select an image; edit or toggle the focused field |
README.md:274: trailing whitespace.
+| `i` in the library | Inspect the highlighted image without changing selection |
README.md:275: trailing whitespace.
+| Space | Optional compatibility alias for selection or checkbox toggling |
README.md:276: trailing whitespace.
+| `/` | Search visible actions, clean catalog or batches |
README.md:277: trailing whitespace.
+| Esc / `0` | Back; discard an unfinished field edit |
README.md:278: trailing whitespace.
+| F1 / F2 / F3 / F4 | Contextual help / pause motion / palette / equivalent direct command |
README.md:279: trailing whitespace.
+| `q` / Ctrl+C | Exit or request a cooperative stop; active source commits finish safely |
README.md:280: trailing whitespace.
+
README.md:281: trailing whitespace.
+Library shortcuts are visible on the screen: `a` selects visible images, `n` clears, `p` opens protection. Forms use `p` for a read-only plan and `r` for the explicit operation; Clean & organize always previews first and also exposes `v` for the audit. The migration result requires a typed **REPLACE** confirmation before source replacement.
README.md:282: trailing whitespace.
+
README.md:283: trailing whitespace.
+Designed terminal sizes include **132×46, 100×34 and 79×24**. Below 72×24, interactive actions are suspended behind a resize notice; exit/cancellation remains available. The line-input alternative does not need raw keyboard mode. `--no-color`, `NO_COLOR`, `--plain`, `--no-animation` and `RIPOSTE_NO_ANIMATION=1` are supported. Session preferences and local model paths are not written to a history file.

+## Direct targeting and publishing
README.md:286: trailing whitespace.
+
README.md:287: trailing whitespace.
+```powershell
README.md:288: trailing whitespace.
+# Read-only listing and image selection filters.
README.md:289: trailing whitespace.
+riposte list ROOT --year 2026 --month September --size MED
README.md:290: trailing whitespace.
+riposte list ROOT --search September --limit 20 --offset 0
README.md:291: trailing whitespace.
+riposte inspect ROOT IMAGE_ID
README.md:292: trailing whitespace.
+
README.md:293: trailing whitespace.
+# Preview the reconstruction and organization; this writes nothing.
README.md:294: trailing whitespace.
+riposte --json organize ROOT --date-mode modified --discard-sidecars
README.md:295: trailing whitespace.
+
README.md:296: trailing whitespace.
+# Review the destinations and copy plan_id; repeat the same options.
README.md:297: trailing whitespace.
+riposte organize ROOT --apply --plan-id PLAN_ID --date-mode modified --discard-sidecars
README.md:298: trailing whitespace.
+riposte audit ROOT
README.md:299: trailing whitespace.
+
README.md:300: trailing whitespace.
+# Preview selected final-size outputs, without a model requirement.
README.md:301: trailing whitespace.
+riposte publish ROOT --all --year 2026 --month 9 --edge 1024 --edge 1536 --plan
README.md:302: trailing whitespace.
+
README.md:303: trailing whitespace.
+# Apply protection to selected clean images. No master image is changed.
README.md:304: trailing whitespace.
+riposte publish ROOT --image IMAGE_ID --vae "D:\Models\Encoder" --edge 1536 --epsilon 4 --steps 24 --restarts 2
README.md:305: trailing whitespace.
+
README.md:306: trailing whitespace.
+# Open exact output bytes, record visual acceptance, then package.
README.md:307: trailing whitespace.
+riposte publications ROOT BATCH_ID
README.md:308: trailing whitespace.
+riposte image ROOT --batch BATCH_ID --item ITEM_ID --open
README.md:309: trailing whitespace.
+riposte review ROOT BATCH_ID --item ITEM_ID --visual-accepted --acknowledge-unverified
README.md:310: trailing whitespace.
+riposte package ROOT BATCH_ID
README.md:311: trailing whitespace.
+
README.md:312: trailing whitespace.
+# Resume unfinished work with matching local models; retain committed outputs.
README.md:313: trailing whitespace.
+riposte resume ROOT BATCH_ID --vae "D:\Models\Encoder"
README.md:314: trailing whitespace.
+```
README.md:315: trailing whitespace.
+
README.md:316: trailing whitespace.
+`ROOT`, `IMAGE_ID`, `BATCH_ID`, and `ITEM_ID` are placeholders; use the path and IDs returned by the application. Repeat `--image`, `--vae`, `--edge` and `--item` where appropriate. Explicit IDs and `--all` are mutually exclusive; date/size filters qualify `--all`. Up to 200 images, four encoders and three distinct publishing edges are accepted. No upscale; duplicate effective output sizes are avoided. `protect` is a typed alias of `publish`.
README.md:317: trailing whitespace.
+
README.md:318: trailing whitespace.
+The reviewed ZIP is written under `.riposte/exports/BATCH_ID/RIPOSTE.zip`. Only accepted date-only PNGs enter it, with exact candidate bytes and the requested year/month/size organization. Model paths, reports, clean comparison images and raw metadata do not enter it. Rejection excludes an output from future packs, not from already downloaded copies.
README.md:319: trailing whitespace.
+
README.md:320: trailing whitespace.
+## Automation is not an animated transcript
README.md:321: trailing whitespace.
+
README.md:322: trailing whitespace.
+```powershell
README.md:323: trailing whitespace.
+riposte --json list ROOT
README.md:324: trailing whitespace.
+riposte --json --events publish ROOT --all --vae "D:\Models\Encoder" --edge 1024
README.md:325: trailing whitespace.
+```
README.md:326: trailing whitespace.
+
README.md:327: trailing whitespace.
+Global presentation flags precede the command. Direct output automatically becomes JSON when stdout is redirected; `--human` forces readable Rich output. `--json` emits one result document on stdout. `--events` sends code-defined progress JSON Lines separately to stderr. Errors return sanitized codes rather than raw exceptions, local paths or metadata. No animation enters machine output.
README.md:328: trailing whitespace.
+
README.md:329: trailing whitespace.
+Exit statuses: **0** completed command, **2** invalid input/operation failure/incomplete checkpoint or blocked output, **130** cooperative model cancellation, **143** handled SIGTERM. A publishing queue reaching its end is not silently converted into success when items are blocked. A non-cancellable source commit returns its actual outcome after Ctrl+C rather than abandoning a half-commit.
README.md:330: trailing whitespace.
+
README.md:331: trailing whitespace.
+## Existing privacy and image contracts
README.md:332: trailing whitespace.
+
README.md:333: trailing whitespace.
+The calendar roots remain `2026`, `2025`, `2024`, `EVERYTHING_ELSE`; English month directories and SML/MED/LRG/XTL appear only when needed. Bins are pixel area below 1M, 4M, 16M and 16M or more. Images are not sorted by file extension.
README.md:334: trailing whitespace.
+
README.md:335: trailing whitespace.
+Sanitation still decodes supported input, applies orientation, converts supported colour profiles, rebuilds pixels, clears fully transparent hidden RGB, and writes fresh PNG with only its date. Applying organization saves private original copies for undo before removing source paths. Video bytes and private recovery copies retain original metadata; date-only sanitation applies to generated images. Unsupported and failed inputs remain blockers. This is not forensic erasure of operating-system caches or data encoded into visible images.
README.md:336: trailing whitespace.

README.md:337: trailing whitespace.
+The protection engines are unchanged in this CLI release and remain **experimental**. Generating a candidate, passing a numerical visual limit, or accepting its appearance does not establish AI resistance. No new efficacy or pretrained-model result is claimed here. There is no watermark, audience login, licensing gate, images upload or noise-only fallback.

docs/CLI.md:5: trailing whitespace.

docs/CLI.md:6: trailing whitespace.
+and API share this check for previews and execution; rejection creates no state.

docs/CLI.md:8: trailing whitespace.
+separate read-only source selection for standalone protection. See the

+## Filename category discovery
docs/CLI.md:73: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:74: trailing whitespace.
+`riposte categories ROOT` discovers recurring words and contiguous phrases in
docs/CLI.md:75: trailing whitespace.
+image/video filename stems. Each term counts once per file, wherever it appears.
docs/CLI.md:76: trailing whitespace.
+Use `--min-count 1` to include one-off terms, `--max-words 1` for words only,
docs/CLI.md:77: trailing whitespace.
+`--ignore TERM` to hide a suggestion, and `--fillers ""` to disable filler words.
docs/CLI.md:78: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:79: trailing whitespace.

docs/CLI.md:80: trailing whitespace.
+riposte categories ROOT --category "Hockey=hockey;goalie;goaltender"
docs/CLI.md:81: trailing whitespace.
+riposte categories ROOT --category "Sports=hockey" --category "Practice=practice"
docs/CLI.md:82: trailing whitespace.
+```
docs/CLI.md:83: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:84: trailing whitespace.
+Each explicit group selects or renames a category and merges its supplied terms.
docs/CLI.md:85: trailing whitespace.
+Repeated groups show overlaps for review without assigning a winner. In the menu,
docs/CLI.md:86: trailing whitespace.
+open Sort and press `c`; edit Category groups and run again to review them.
docs/CLI.md:87: trailing whitespace.
+Separate groups with commas in the form. The report shows matching-file counts
docs/CLI.md:88: trailing whitespace.
+and title examples; Details retains the complete bounded report.
docs/CLI.md:89: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:90: trailing whitespace.

docs/CLI.md:91: trailing whitespace.

docs/CLI.md:92: trailing whitespace.
+the separate command below. Exact destinations and private title provenance are available through the

+## Saved category rules
docs/CLI.md:96: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:97: trailing whitespace.
+```powershell
docs/CLI.md:98: trailing whitespace.
+# Preview a complete replacement ruleset and its filename matches. Writes nothing.
docs/CLI.md:99: trailing whitespace.
+riposte category-rules ROOT --category "Hockey=hockey;goalie;goaltender" --exclude "Hockey=table hockey;toy" --priority "Hockey=10" --review
docs/CLI.md:100: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:101: trailing whitespace.
+# First save: use the same selection and the revision from the preview.
docs/CLI.md:102: trailing whitespace.
+riposte category-rules ROOT --category "Hockey=hockey;goalie;goaltender" --exclude "Hockey=table hockey;toy" --priority "Hockey=10" --save --expected-revision missing
docs/CLI.md:103: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:104: trailing whitespace.
+# Reload the saved rules and review their matches without changing files.
docs/CLI.md:105: trailing whitespace.
+riposte category-rules ROOT --review
docs/CLI.md:106: trailing whitespace.
+```
docs/CLI.md:107: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:108: trailing whitespace.
+Subsequent saves require the full revision printed by a fresh preview; a changed
docs/CLI.md:109: trailing whitespace.
+ruleset stops the save. Saving replaces the **complete** category selection, so
docs/CLI.md:110: trailing whitespace.
+repeat `--category` for every group to retain. `--clear` previews an empty ruleset;
docs/CLI.md:111: trailing whitespace.
+combine it with `--save --expected-revision REVISION` to clear saved rules.
docs/CLI.md:112: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:113: trailing whitespace.
+Aliases and phrases match complete normalized words wherever they occur in the
docs/CLI.md:114: trailing whitespace.
+filename stem. Exclusions apply to that category alone. Higher priority wins;
docs/CLI.md:118: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:119: trailing whitespace.
+Folder slugs default to normalized category labels joined with hyphens. Use
docs/CLI.md:120: trailing whitespace.
+`--slug "New label=existing-slug"` to retain a folder identity when renaming a
docs/CLI.md:121: trailing whitespace.
+label. Duplicate slugs, Windows device names and unsafe path characters are
docs/CLI.md:124: trailing whitespace.
+supported. Rules allow up to 32 categories, 32 included/excluded phrases each,
docs/CLI.md:125: trailing whitespace.
+and priorities from -1,000,000 to 1,000,000. Phrases have one to three words.
docs/CLI.md:126: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:127: trailing whitespace.
+Only the rules are persisted, in private `.riposte/category-rules.json` (or the
docs/CLI.md:128: trailing whitespace.
+existing legacy `.shroud` namespace). Filename samples and review assignments
docs/CLI.md:129: trailing whitespace.
+are not saved by this command. In the terminal Sort form press `s` to review
docs/CLI.md:130: trailing whitespace.
+and save rules with a separate `SAVE` confirmation. The browser sorting dialog
docs/CLI.md:131: trailing whitespace.
+also offers discovery, rule editing, review and explicit save.

docs/CLI.md:149: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:151: trailing whitespace.
+`2025/March/img/hockey/SML/SML01.png`. A category plan shows source path, original
docs/CLI.md:152: trailing whitespace.
+filename (unknown for old untracked files), matched terms, category and exact
docs/CLI.md:153: trailing whitespace.
+numbered destination. Minimum discovery counts do not limit saved-rule matches.

+

docs/CLI.md:160: trailing whitespace.

docs/CLI.md:161: trailing whitespace.

docs/CLI.md:162: trailing whitespace.
+the reviewed plan. The terminal accepts a JSON list for this field, preserving
docs/CLI.md:163: trailing whitespace.

docs/CLI.md:165: trailing whitespace.

docs/CLI.md:166: trailing whitespace.

docs/CLI.md:167: trailing whitespace.
+again. Late changes during an operation stop it with an incomplete checkpoint;
docs/CLI.md:168: trailing whitespace.
+private undo history retains recorded work for recovery. Existing files are never
docs/CLI.md:169: trailing whitespace.
+overwritten or assigned a different destination after approval.
docs/CLI.md:170: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:171: trailing whitespace.
+New sorts retain trustworthy original basenames privately, including normal sorts
docs/CLI.md:172: trailing whitespace.
+performed before enabling categories. Media IDs survive moves and identical bytes
docs/CLI.md:173: trailing whitespace.
+in different files keep separate identities. Existing catalogs remain readable;
docs/CLI.md:177: trailing whitespace.

+## Publishing configuration
docs/CLI.md:192: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:193: trailing whitespace.
+`publish` accepts repeated `--image`, repeated `--vae` (up to four), repeated `--edge` (up to three), `--all`, `--year`, `--month`, `--size`, `--plan`, `--epsilon`, `--steps`, `--restarts`, `--seed`, `--device`, `--inpaint`, `--video`, `--preserve-white/--no-preserve-white`, `--delta-e76-p95`, `--rms-fraction`.
docs/CLI.md:194: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:195: trailing whitespace.
+A model-dependent action with no usable model stops with a code; it does not add a cosmetic effect instead. Local paths are reselected on a later session because RIPOSTE does not save a model-path history. Four local encoders are not a promise that four large models fit available memory.
docs/CLI.md:196: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:197: trailing whitespace.
+`--all` means all matching catalog images. For unorganized inputs, use repeated `--input PATH` with `riposte`, `publish`, or `protect`; a path can be a file or a recursively scanned directory. Relative paths use the current working directory. At most 200 distinct files are selected; overlapping paths are deduplicated. Non-image files are left untouched, corrupt image inputs stop intake, links/junctions are rejected, and internal `.riposte`/`.shroud` directories are excluded. Raw paths and metadata are not saved in batch records. `--date-mode download|modified|embedded` chooses the snapshot date basis. Originals stay where they are.
docs/CLI.md:198: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:199: trailing whitespace.
+Use exactly one selection style: `--input`, `--image`, or `--all`. Date and resolution filters qualify `--all`; they cannot be combined with raw inputs or explicit IDs. Selected catalog sources are verified individually, so unrelated additions do not force sorting before a protection run.
docs/CLI.md:200: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:201: trailing whitespace.
+```powershell
docs/CLI.md:202: trailing whitespace.
+# Repeat only sorting when new images arrives.
docs/CLI.md:203: trailing whitespace.
+riposte sort ROOT
docs/CLI.md:204: trailing whitespace.
+riposte sort ROOT --apply --plan-id PLAN_ID
docs/CLI.md:205: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:206: trailing whitespace.
+# Inspect recovery before explicitly restoring the last recorded sort.
docs/CLI.md:207: trailing whitespace.
+riposte undo-sort ROOT
docs/CLI.md:208: trailing whitespace.
+riposte undo-sort ROOT --apply
docs/CLI.md:209: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:210: trailing whitespace.
+# Protect selected originals without restructuring their directory.
docs/CLI.md:211: trailing whitespace.
+riposte riposte ROOT --input "D:\Images\drawing.png" --input "D:\Images\New" --plan
docs/CLI.md:212: trailing whitespace.
+riposte riposte ROOT --input "D:\Images\drawing.png" --input "D:\Images\New" --vae MODEL
docs/CLI.md:213: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:214: trailing whitespace.
+# Preview both operations, then explicitly authorize source replacement.
docs/CLI.md:215: trailing whitespace.
+riposte sort-riposte ROOT --all --vae MODEL
docs/CLI.md:216: trailing whitespace.
+riposte sort-riposte ROOT --all --vae MODEL --apply --plan-id COMBINED_PLAN_ID
docs/CLI.md:217: trailing whitespace.
+```
docs/CLI.md:218: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:229: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:230: trailing whitespace.
+The combined command sorts the whole `ROOT`; it does not accept `--input`. Its protection selection is `--all` (optionally filtered) or existing `--image` IDs. `--discard-sidecars` is separate consent to delete metadata sidecars during sorting. Model preflight occurs before source replacement. An incomplete sort stops the pipeline; a later protection failure keeps the sorting receipt and supports a separate RIPOSTE retry. A created batch uses the existing `resume`, exact-pixel `review`, and `package` commands. Neither workflow approves images automatically.
docs/CLI.md:231: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:232: trailing whitespace.
+Sort destinations are `Year/Month/img/Size` and `Year/Month/vid/Size`. Video
docs/CLI.md:233: trailing whitespace.
+inspection requires ffprobe on PATH. Video files retain their bytes and metadata
docs/CLI.md:234: trailing whitespace.
+and never enter the image protection catalog. Original files and explicitly
docs/CLI.md:235: trailing whitespace.
+discarded sidecars are retained privately for undo, including their original
docs/CLI.md:236: trailing whitespace.
+metadata and names. This uses extra disk space. Undo requires these recovery
docs/CLI.md:237: trailing whitespace.
+copies and refuses conflicts; it cannot recover sorts made before history was
docs/CLI.md:238: trailing whitespace.
+introduced. `Undo last sort` is a main-menu action and the Sort form's `u` key.
docs/CLI.md:239: trailing whitespace.
+Its preview must be followed by the exact `UNDO` word. Protection results remain
docs/CLI.md:240: trailing whitespace.
+in place. Interrupted recovery must be completed before another sort.
docs/CLI.md:241: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:242: trailing whitespace.
+The workshop guard retains `--engine coupled|encoder`; the primary publish route calls the inherited release-v1 algorithm. A workshop pipeline is selected with `--pipeline`; primary publishing uses `--inpaint` and `--video`.
docs/CLI.md:243: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:244: trailing whitespace.
+## Presentation and terminal capabilities
docs/CLI.md:245: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:246: trailing whitespace.
+```text
docs/CLI.md:247: trailing whitespace.
+riposte --json COMMAND ...
docs/CLI.md:248: trailing whitespace.
+riposte --human COMMAND ...
docs/CLI.md:249: trailing whitespace.
+riposte --plain COMMAND ...
docs/CLI.md:250: trailing whitespace.
+riposte --events --json COMMAND ...
docs/CLI.md:251: trailing whitespace.
+riposte --no-animation ui ROOT
docs/CLI.md:252: trailing whitespace.
+riposte --theme goks ui ROOT
docs/CLI.md:253: trailing whitespace.
+riposte --theme light ui ROOT
docs/CLI.md:254: trailing whitespace.
+riposte --theme mono --fps 12 ui ROOT
docs/CLI.md:255: trailing whitespace.
+riposte --no-color ui ROOT
docs/CLI.md:256: trailing whitespace.
+```
docs/CLI.md:257: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:258: trailing whitespace.
+These are global flags: put them before the command. stdout redirection automatically selects JSON for direct commands. Diagnostic/operation events are on stderr. `--plain` suppresses ANSI and selects the linear interface on an interactive entry. `TERM=dumb` also uses linear menus. `NO_COLOR` suppresses colours without deleting keyboard functionality. `RIPOSTE_NO_ANIMATION=1` freezes authored motion; actual time and callback counts remain live. ASCII glyph output does not require a special terminal font. Local Unicode path input is retained, while the ASCII display uses a replacement glyph for non-ASCII characters.
docs/CLI.md:259: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:260: trailing whitespace.
+The terminal uses Rich Live's alternate screen. On ordinary exit, Ctrl+C, handled errors, or handled SIGTERM it restores terminal mode, bracketed-paste state and cursor visibility and joins the owned worker. SIGKILL, kernel failure and power loss cannot run cleanup. A particular model forward call cannot be forcibly interrupted safely; cancellation waits for a backend checkpoint. Source replacement, review and package commits are not abandoned on an interactive interrupt.
docs/CLI.md:261: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:262: trailing whitespace.
+## Result display and contextual controls (0.5)
docs/CLI.md:263: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:264: trailing whitespace.
+Normal results are semantic reports, shared by the interactive and direct human CLI. `t` cycles Overview, complete Details and syntax-coloured JSON. Only explicit machine output retains raw JSON as its primary representation. Backgrounds always belong to the host terminal.
docs/CLI.md:265: trailing whitespace.

docs/CLI.md:266: trailing whitespace.

+## Interactive actions
docs/CLI.md:269: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:270: trailing whitespace.
+Numbered actions and arrow/Enter navigation are always visible. The home screen exposes RIPOSTE, Sort, and Sort + RIPOSTE and keeps the workshop last. `Esc` or `0` returns without changing a field; `q` exits outside a text field. Bracketed paste is consumed as field/search data, not as queued Enter or menu shortcuts. Input and rendering never evaluate arbitrary shell text. RIPOSTE first browses selected files/folders, then asks where to save results. The input selection is not limited to four paths. `f` reveals advanced protection settings without discarding the selection. Both Sort forms always preview first and require the exact word `REPLACE` before source replacement; protection-only forms never sort.
docs/CLI.md:271: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:272: trailing whitespace.
+The image library supports search, multi-selection and selection-preserving inspection. Its `p` shortcut carries that selection into protection settings. Model directories entered in Local models remain available in the current process's publishing and helper forms. `F4` shows the configured command; in a publishing form it shows the default read-only preview invocation. After an operation it shows that actual operation. Copying/executing it is the user's separate action; RIPOSTE performs no clipboard write or shell evaluation.
docs/CLI.md:273: trailing whitespace.
+
docs/CLI.md:274: trailing whitespace.
+The source-migration preview shows the actual current plan, then `a` opens the explicit **REPLACE** boundary. Apply re-inspects the workspace and rejects a changed receipt before state initialization. Execution uses only the reviewed files and destinations. Later additions are retained and reported as incomplete; collisions never choose an unreviewed replacement destination. Ordinary sorting can still apply valid files from an unchanged plan with blockers, retaining undo for recorded changes. Category and combined sorting require a ready plan. Output acceptance has its own **ACCEPT** boundary and both underlying acknowledgements; a local viewer launch is not an acceptance event. The panel's numerical output checks are not an AI protection percentage.
